TL;DR: 本文提出并压力测试了1-consistency方法（基于多个独立训练的人脸识别模型在排名第一的候选身份上是否一致来判定匹配与否），在36种不同场景下与两种分数阈值方法（固定阈值FST和理论最优阈值OST）对比。结果表明1-consistency无需提前设定阈值、无需预知探针质量，在退化探针场景下达到了与理论最优阈值方法相当的准确率，且其标记为匹配时正确率高达97-100%，远优于OST的66-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在1:N人脸识别中，判断探针是否已在图库中注册至关重要但极具挑战性：拒绝匹配会丢失有效线索，接受非匹配则导致误判甚至冤案。传统的分数阈值方法在图像质量和图库规模变化时表现脆弱，因为分数分布会随之显著偏移，但部署前无法预知实际运行条件。

Method: 采用1-consistency方法：多个独立训练的人脸识别模型对同一探针分别进行排名，若所有模型返回的第一名身份一致，则判定为匹配（MP）；否则判定为非匹配（MA）。在36种（图库大小×图像质量）场景下进行压力测试，对比FST（固定阈值，在基线条件下校准一次）和OST（每个场景重新优化的理论最优阈值）。

Result: FST在质量退化时性能严重不对称崩溃（MP召回率降至2%以下，MA召回率保持近100%）。OST作为理论最优阈值基准，在退化探针场景下1-consistency无需任何调优即达到了与之相当的准确率。关键的差异在于：1-consistency标记为MP时，正确匹配率高达97-100%，而OST在严重退化下仅为66-84%。

Conclusion: 1-consistency无需设定任何阈值，因此不需要提前知道探针到达时的条件（图像质量、图库组成），在实际部署中可实现与理论最优阈值方法相当甚至更优的性能，是一种真正可用的开放集人脸识别方法。

Abstract: In operational 1:N face identification, a crucial question arises for each probe: is this person enrolled in the gallery or not? The stakes are high and asymmetric. Rejecting a mate-present (MP) probe loses a valid lead; accepting a mate-absent (MA) probe makes every returned candidate a false identification, at worst a wrongful arrest. Most approaches threshold match scores, but scores shift substantially with image quality and gallery size and composition, making thresholds fixed before deployment brittle under realistic conditions. Our prior work introduced 1-consistency, the only method based on rank consensus across multiple independently trained matchers: a probe is labeled MP if all matchers return the same rank-1 identity. This work stress-tests 1-consistency across 36 (gallery, probe quality) scenarios spanning four quality levels and two structural axes: images per identity and total enrolled identities. We benchmark against two score-thresholding methods that bracket what any deployed threshold could achieve. Fixed Score-Thresholding (FST), calibrated once on baseline conditions, collapses asymmetrically as quality degrades: MP recall falls below 2% while MA recall holds near 100%. Oracle Score-Thresholding (OST), re-tuned per scenario, is the best any threshold could theoretically do, yet for degraded probes 1-consistency matches it with zero tuning. The two differ mainly in error type (OST favors MP recall, 1-consistency favors MA recall), but on one axis 1-consistency does not merely match the oracle: when it labels a probe MP, it returns the correct mate 97-100% of the time versus OST's 66-84% under severe degradation. In short, 1-consistency delivers oracle-level accuracy without the impossible requirement: it sets no threshold, so it needs no advance knowledge of the conditions a probe will arrive in, which is what makes it usable.

</details>


### [90] [Open-KNEAD: Knowledge-grounded Nutrition Estimation via Agentic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12911)
*Bruce Coburn,Jingbo Yue,Jinge Ma,Siddeshwar Raghavan,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-KNEAD是一个无需训练、可本地部署的知识驱动智能体框架，用于从单张未标注的餐食图像中进行营养估算。它通过将每个食物项分解并匹配到FNDDS数据库，在保留可审计记录的同时提高了份量估算精度，且在多个基准测试中优于直接估算和先前的检索增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然检索增强接地（retrieval-augmented grounding）曾被证明能提升MLLM的营养估算，但现代MLLM的直接估算已匹配或超越了完整检索流水线。然而，临床医生仍然需要准确的份量估算和可追溯的逐项记录。因此，本文旨在探索：在检索不再提升整体估算的情况下，是否仍能在保持低用户负担（单张未标注图像）、可解释性（可审计记录）和隐私性（本地推理）的前提下，实现这两项临床价值。

Method: 提出Open-KNEAD框架，采用智能体（agent）方式将餐食图像分解为单个食物项，通过选择性、营养感知的检索将每个项映射到FNDDS编码，并引入食谱先验步骤来恢复烹饪添加的能量（针对非美国菜系）。整个框架无需训练，可本地部署。

Result: 在两种开源MLLM家族和三种菜系上，Open-KNEAD在大多数骨干网络-数据集设置下优于先前的接地方法和直接估算。在由营养师验证的ACETADA数据集上，本地开源智能体的份量估算比两个前沿闭源模型分别高出约30%和53%。

Conclusion: 检索增强虽然不再提升MLLM的整体营养估算，但通过Open-KNEAD这种知识驱动的智能体分解与接地方法，仍能显著改善份量估算精度，同时提供可审计的逐项记录，且完全可在本地部署，满足临床采纳的关键需求。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for dietary assessment from meal images, where retrieval-augmented grounding was shown to sharpen nutrition estimates. However, we find this premise no longer holds for current MLLMs. A modern MLLM's direct estimate now matches or surpasses the full retrieval pipeline. This raises a question: if retrieval no longer improves the overall estimate, can it still deliver the two things clinicians value, accurate portions and a traceable, item-by-item record? We pursue this while preserving what matters for clinical adoption: minimal user burden (a single, unannotated meal image), explainability (an auditable record), and privacy (locally hosted inference). We introduce Open-KNEAD, a knowledge-grounded agentic framework for meal nutrition estimation that is training-free and locally deployable. Each decomposed food item is grounded to a Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies (FNDDS) code via selective, nutrient-aware retrieval, composing an auditable per-item record. Across two open MLLM families and three cuisines, Open-KNEAD improves portion estimates over both prior grounding methods and direct estimation in most backbone-dataset settings. An agent-internal recipe-prior step further recovers the invisible cooking-added energy that biases estimates on non-US cuisine. The advantage is largest on the dietitian-verified ACETADA dataset, where the local open agent surpasses the direct portion estimates of two frontier closed models by roughly $30\%$ and $53\%$, all while keeping every meal image on local hardware. We release the Open-KNEAD framework and its agent-ready FNDDS knowledge base.

</details>


### [91] [Domain-Incremental Remote Sensing Change Detection via Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12934)
*Daifeng Peng,Yaning Li,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DG-FDD框架，结合差异引导自适应（DGDA）与频率解耦蒸馏（FDKD-CS），解决遥感变化检测模型在域增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个数据集上实现了接近独立训练单任务模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域增量学习方法主要保留图像级表征，忽略了双时相差异线索，而该线索对域偏移下的鲁棒变化检测至关重要。

Method: 提出DG-FDD框架：1) 差异引导动态适配器（DGDA）建模双时相特征差异，促进变化感知特征适配并减少域特定干扰；2) 频率解耦知识蒸馏与跨域合成策略（FDKD-CS）在频域分离结构信息与域风格，实现无需历史数据的稳定知识迁移。

Result: 在6个两域序列上，与独立单任务模型相比，F1和IoU平均相对变化仅为-0.23%和-0.45%；在3个三域序列上为-0.69%和-1.31%。

Conclusion: DG-FDD有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，在历史知识保留与新域适应之间取得了良好的稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (RSCD) models are prone to catastrophic forgetting when incrementally adapted to new domains. Existing domain-incremental learning (DIL) methods mainly preserve image-level representations but often overlook bitemporal discrepancy cues, which are critical for robust change detection under domain shifts. To address this limitation, we propose DG-FDD, a domain-incremental change detection framework that integrates Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation. Specifically, the Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter (DGDA) models bitemporal feature discrepancies to promote change-aware feature adaptation and reduce domain-specific interference. Meanwhile, the Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation strategy with Cross-domain Synthesis (FDKD-CS) separates structural information from domain style in the frequency domain, enabling stable knowledge transfer without historical data. Extensive experiments on three public high-resolution RSCD datasets under two- and three-domain incremental protocols demonstrate that DG-FDD effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting. Compared with independently trained single-task models, DG-FDD records mean relative changes in F1 and IoU of only -0.23% and -0.45%, respectively, across six two-domain sequences, and -0.69% and -1.31%, respectively, across the three evaluated three-domain sequences. These results indicate a favorable stability-plasticity balance between historical knowledge retention and new-domain adaptation in continual cross-domain change detection.

</details>


### [92] [Point Tracking in Surgery--The 2025 Surgical Tattoos in Infrared Challenge (STIRC2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12939)
*Adam Schmidt,Mert Asim Karaoglu,Zijian Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Tim Salcudean,Ho-Gun Ha,Minkang Jang,Kyungmin Jung,Ihsan Ullah,Hyunki Lee,Suresh Guttikonda,Sarah Latus,Alexander Schlaefer,Xinkai Zhao,Yuichiro Hayashi,Masahiro Oda,Takayuki Kitasaka,Kensaku Mori,Peng Liu,Chenyang Li,Stefanie Speidel,Aoife Gardiner,Agostino Stilli,Danail Stoyanov,Francisco Vasconcelos,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Van Nguyen Nguyen,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu,Alexander Ladikos,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2025年外科手术点跟踪挑战赛（STIRC2025），该挑战赛使用STIR数据集评估算法在手术视频中追踪点的准确性和效率，共有7支团队参与，论文总结了比赛结果和参赛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术中的点跟踪对于下游任务（如分割、3D重建、虚拟组织标记、自主探头扫描和子任务自主性）至关重要，但目前缺乏标准化的评估基准，因此需要组织挑战赛来量化评估算法性能。

Method: 挑战赛使用名为STIR（外科手术红外标记）的数据集，从准确性和效率两个维度评估算法。准确性测试基于体内和体外序列，效率测试则衡量算法推理延迟。挑战赛作为MICCAI EndoVis 2025的一部分举办，共7支团队参与。

Result: 7支团队参与了挑战赛，论文总结了各参赛方法的性能表现及最终挑战结果。

Conclusion: STIRC2025为外科手术点跟踪提供了标准化的评估基准，数据集和基线代码已公开，可供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Point tracking in surgery is crucial to enable applications in downstream tasks such as segmentation, 3D reconstruction, virtual tissue landmarking, autonomous probe-based scanning, and subtask autonomy. This paper introduces the 2025 iteration of a point tracking challenge to address this, wherein participants submit their algorithms for quantification. Their algorithms are evaluated using a dataset named surgical tattoos in infrared (STIR), with the challenge named the STIR Challenge 2025 (STIRC2025). The STIR Challenge 2025 comprises two quantitative components: accuracy and efficiency. The accuracy component tests the accuracy of algorithms on in vivo and ex vivo sequences. The efficiency component tests algorithm inference latency. The challenge was conducted as a part of MICCAI EndoVis 2025, and seven teams participated in this challenge. In this paper we summarize the challenge results and participant methods. The challenge dataset is available at: https://zenodo.org/records/20191078, and the code for baseline models and metrics calculation is available here: https://github.com/athaddius/STIRMetrics

</details>


### [93] [ViCo3D: Empowering LiDAR-based Collaborative 3D Object Detection with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12959)
*Haojie Ren,Songrui Luo,Lingfeng Wang,Yan Xia,Yao Li,Jing Li,Lu Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViCo3D是一种利用视觉基础模型（VFM）增强LiDAR BEV表示的协同3D检测框架，通过将点云投影为三通道BEV图像、多尺度融合模块和自车跨智能体融合策略，在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet上达到SOTA性能，协同增益较先前方法提升达1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LiDAR的V2X协同3D感知中，BEV表示由从头训练的LiDAR骨干提取，以几何信息为主，缺乏通用语义先验，限制了特征级协同的效果。视觉基础模型（VFM）在大规模图像数据上预训练后具备强大的通用视觉表征能力，有望增强LiDAR BEV表示，但因图像与点云间的模态差距难以直接适配。

Method: 1) 将点云投影到BEV平面作为三通道图像，使DINOv2提取BEV空间视觉特征；2) 在单智能体编码器中引入多尺度BEV融合模块，整合DINOv2特征与LiDAR几何特征；3) 采用以自车为中心的跨智能体融合策略聚合多智能体互补信息。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet数据集上达到SOTA 3D检测性能，在DAIR-V2X上协同增益较先前方法提升最高达1.8倍。

Conclusion: ViCo3D有效弥合了图像与点云间的模态差距，成功将VFM适配到LiDAR协同感知中，显著提升了协同3D检测性能。

Abstract: LiDAR-based collaborative 3D perception in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) systems typically relies on fusing bird's-eye-view (BEV) features across agents. However, current BEV representations, typically extracted by LiDAR backbones trained from scratch, are geometry-dominated and lack general semantic priors, inherently limiting the efficacy of feature-level collaboration. Meanwhile, vision foundation models (VFMs) pretrained on large-scale image data have demonstrated strong capability in learning general-purpose and informative visual representations for 2D tasks, and have the potential to enhance agent-wise LiDAR BEV representations for collaboration. Despite this potential, adapting VFMs to LiDAR-based 3D detection remains challenging due to the substantial image-point cloud modality gap. To bridge this gap, we propose ViCo3D, a collaborative 3D object detection framework powered by VFMs. Specifically, ViCo3D adapts VFMs to LiDAR-based collaborative perception from three aspects: First, ViCo3D projects point clouds onto the BEV plane as three-channel images, enabling DINOv2 to extract BEV-space visual features from LiDAR inputs. Besides, to effectively integrate these DINOv2-derived features with LiDAR geometric features, ViCo3D introduces a multi-scale BEV fusion module within the single-agent encoder. In addition, ViCo3D adopts an ego-centric cross-agent fusion strategy to aggregate complementary information from multiple agents. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XSet demonstrate that ViCo3D achieves state-of-the-art 3D detection performance. Remarkably, it delivers up to 1.8x greater collaborative gains than prior methods on DAIR-V2X. The code will be made public available for future investigation.

</details>


### [94] [Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery for Fair and Efficient Malignancy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12987)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出cgDDI混合框架，通过可控生成多样化皮肤病图像（健康样本合成、罕见病灶迁移、小样本参数化生成），在DDI和F17k数据集上实现公平性提升的SOTA性能（90.9%准确率），并开源266k+合成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病诊断需要跨人群的公平性能，但缺乏专业标注图像（尤其深肤色和罕见病）阻碍了公平方法的发展。

Method: cgDDI混合框架： (1) 合成健康皮肤样本而不干扰其他属性； (2) 将单样本罕见病灶非参数化迁移到新肤色和新位置； (3) 支持最少10个训练样本的高效参数化生成。支持人工和自动分割掩码，可扩展至无预置掩码的数据集。

Result: DDI基准：合成训练86.4%准确率，微调后达到90.9% SOTA；F17k跨数据集实验提升+13.9%准确率；将656图像数据集扩展400倍以上。

Conclusion: cgDDI通过可控生成有效缓解数据稀缺和肤色偏差问题，开源资源可促进皮肤病公平性研究。

Abstract: Accurate dermatological diagnosis naturally necessitates equitable performance across diverse populations, yet a systematic lack of expertly annotated images, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, impedes progress toward measurably fair methods. We introduce cgDDI (Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery), a hybrid framework that (1) synthesizes realistic healthy skin samples without disturbing other input properties, (2) maps single-sample rare lesions onto novel skin-tones and locations non-parametrically, and (3) allows for efficient parametric generation with as few as 10 training samples. The framework supports both human and automated segmentation masking, enabling scalability to datasets without pre-made lesion masks. We grow a 656-image dataset by more than 400x and validate across two datasets: biopsy-confirmed Diverse Dermatology Images (DDI) and expert-verified Fitzpatrick17k (F17k). On the DDI benchmark, we achieve malignancy classification accuracy of 86.4% under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, alongside leading fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show +13.9% accuracy improvements on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap. We openly release 266k+ synthetic images, code, and generative models to further support fairness research at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI.

</details>


### [95] [X-Lens: Real-Time Metric Depth Estimation with Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12993)
*Heng Zhou,Shuhong Liu,Yonghao He,Bohao Zhang,Fa Fu,Chenhui Hou,Xianbao Hou,Lijun Han,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-lens是一个紧凑的前馈模型，支持从可变数量的鱼眼和针孔相机视角进行度量深度估计。它通过可学习校准令牌和雅可比参数化畸变偏置实现异构相机间的几何一致性，仅用0.04B参数即可达到41 FPS的实时性能，并在新发布的大型合成数据集OmniScene（266K帧、1.7M图像）上训练，在异构相机度量深度任务上AbsRel降低25.4%，参数减少88.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量深度估计方法通常针对单一相机类型（鱼眼或针孔），无法高效支持异构相机系统（混合鱼眼和针孔）的实时感知需求。同时，依赖辅助重构目标的方法增加了计算和优化复杂度。

Method: 提出几何感知的异构相机公式，包含两个关键组件：(1) 可学习校准令牌，用于粗略对齐鱼眼和针孔投影空间；(2) 雅可比参数化的畸变偏置，注入交叉注意力机制中以建模局部投影变化并促进跨相机一致性。模型直接预测密集深度和全局度量尺度，无需辅助重构目标。

Result: 在OmniScene-Full数据集上，AbsRel相比最强基线降低25.4%，参数减少88.9%。在传统鱼眼-only和针孔-only设置中也达到了竞争性能。模型仅0.04B参数，推理速度达41 FPS。

Conclusion: X-lens以极小的参数量实现了实时异构相机度量深度估计，在多种数据集上验证了其跨相机泛化能力和优越性能。

Abstract: We present X-lens, a compact feed-forward model for metric depth estimation from a variable number of calibrated fisheye and pinhole views. To support real-time downstream perception, X-lens is built around a geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation with two key components. Learnable calibration tokens provide a coarse alignment between fisheye and pinhole projective spaces, while a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias injected into cross-attention models local projection changes and promotes cross-camera consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. The model predicts dense depth together with a global metric scale, avoiding auxiliary reconstruction targets that increase computation and optimization complexity. To learn such cross-camera generalization at scale and depth, X-lens is trained on multiple public datasets and OmniScene, our newly released large-scale synthetic dataset containing approximately 266K synchronized six-view frames, 1.7M individual images, and 103 indoor and outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate superior heterogeneous-camera metric depth accuracy, reducing AbsRel by 25.4\% on OmniScene-Full over the strongest baseline while using 88.9\% fewer parameters, with competitive performance on conventional fisheye-only and pinhole-only settings.

</details>


### [96] [DermDepth: Toward Monocular Metric Scale 3D Reconstruction Models for Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13010)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DermDepth，首个面向皮肤病学领域的单视图度量尺度3D重建模型，并构建了像素级完美3D信息的合成皮肤镜数据集D-Synth。通过在合成数据上训练，将真实皮肤镜数据的度量尺度误差从16倍以上降至1.1倍以内，并在多个真实临床基准上实现跨尺度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤科临床实践中，病灶大小、形态和纹理的测量与追踪至关重要，但现有研究过度聚焦于2D方法，而3D信息能更自然地服务于这些目标。现有3D方法需要额外硬件或多次采集，缺乏单视图度量尺度3D重建方法。

Method: 提出DermDepth单视图度量尺度3D模型和D-Synth合成皮肤镜数据集。在合成数据上训练模型以校正度量尺度误差，并在少量真实临床样本上微调实现泛化。

Result: 训练DermDepth于D-Synth将真实皮肤镜数据的度量尺度误差从16倍以上降至1.1倍以下，同时保持几何质量并提升纹理丰富度。在三个真实基准上（覆盖毫米到百厘米范围、多种肤色、慢性伤口）实现泛化，测量结果与医学文献报道的疾病大小基本一致。

Conclusion: 单视图密集3D重建、度量尺度测量和表面法线纹理估计可以在无需额外硬件或多次采集的情况下实现，适用于皮肤镜和宏观病例，为皮肤科临床提供了实用的3D信息工具。

Abstract: Dermatological practice routinely involves measuring and tracking lesion size, morphology and texture, as critical components of wound or skin cancer screening, monitoring and diagnosis. To accomplish this task, practitioners often image the skin surface with commonly available off-the-shelf camera sensors. This has led to an overwhelming research focus on 2D methods while these objectives naturally benefit from 3D information. In this paper, we demonstrate that dense monocular 3D reconstructions, metric scale measurements and rich surface normal texture estimates are achievable for both dermoscopic and macroscopic cases without the need for additional hardware or multiple captures. We present DermDepth, the first single-view metric scale 3D model for the dermatological domain and D-Synth, the first synthetic dermoscopic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D information. Our experiments show training DermDepth on D-Synth corrects metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x for real dermoscopic data, while preserving geometric quality and increasing texture richness. Fine-tuning on a small amount of real clinical samples generalizes our method across three real-world benchmarks spanning the few mm to hundred cm range, diverse skin-tones, chronic wound cases and produces measurements broadly consistent with disease size reported in medical literature. All code, data and models are available at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Scaling Point-in-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11889)
*Bryan Kelly,Semyon Malamud,Johannes Schwab,Teng Andrea Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过训练多达40亿参数、1万亿时间过滤tokens的decoder-only transformer，构建了2013-2024年的月度模型检查点序列，成功缩小了点位时间语言模型与无约束模型之间的性能差距，并开源完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在无限制互联网语料上训练会嵌入未来信息，产生前瞻偏差，破坏金融和社会科学中回测与因果推断的有效性。点位时间语言模型虽能消除此问题，但现有模型性能远落后于无约束模型，亟需缩小这一差距。

Method: 训练decoder-only transformer模型，参数量高达40亿，使用来源于FineWeb的1万亿按时间顺序过滤的token；构建2013-2024年间的月度模型检查点序列；并通过LoRA进行指令微调以提升下游可用性。

Result: 在常识推理和语言理解基准上，所提模型性能接近同规模领先开源模型（如Gemma-3-4B和LLaMA-7B），但在部分任务上仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 通过扩大规模可显著缩小点位时间语言模型与无约束模型的性能差距；开源完整流程（数据集构建、训练基础设施、评估代码）以支持可复现的点位时间语言建模及需要严格时间有效性的研究应用。

Abstract: Large language models trained on unrestricted internet corpora inevitably embed information from the future, introducing lookahead bias that compromises the validity of backtests and causal inference in finance and the social sciences. Point-in-time language models--trained exclusively on text available up to each calendar date--eliminate this leakage by construction, but existing efforts typically produce models that lag substantially behind their unconstrained counterparts. We show that this performance gap can be substantially narrowed through scale. Training decoder-only transformers with up to 4 billion parameters on 1 trillion chronologically filtered tokens from FineWeb, we construct a sequence of monthly model checkpoints spanning 2013-2024. Across a range of common-sense reasoning and language understanding benchmarks, our models approach the performance of leading open-weight models of comparable size (e.g., Gemma-3-4B and LLaMA-7B) trained on temporally unrestricted data, although a performance gap remains on several tasks. Instruction fine-tuning via LoRA further improves downstream usability. We release the complete pipeline--including dataset construction, training infrastructure, and evaluation code--to enable reproducible point-in-time language modeling and to support research applications that require strict temporal validity.

</details>


### [98] [CANDI: Contextual Alignment for Niche Domains Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11891)
*Megha Chakraborty,Darssan L. Eswaramoorthi,Het Riteshkumar Shah,Madhur Thareja,Michelle A Ihetu,Harshul Raj Surana,Kaushik Roy,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CANDI-QA是一个专门用于评估大语言模型在专业领域（如医疗诊断和金融咨询）中上下文对齐能力的新型基准数据集。该数据集包含信息辅助和应用推理两类问题，并提出了轻量级神经符号框架MTSS-Net作为基线。实验表明，当前LLM在专业领域的上下文对齐方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问答基准无法捕捉专业领域所需的细微上下文基础、用户意识和领域理解能力，亟需一个专门评估LLM在专业环境中生成准确、上下文敏感且与用户对齐答案的数据集。

Method: 构建CANDI-QA数据集，包含专家策划的两类问答对：信息辅助问题（直接事实查询）和应用推理问题（多跳推理）。评估十余种语言模型，并提出MTSS-Net轻量级神经符号框架（结合神经检索与规则推理）作为基线。

Result: 当前LLM在专业领域的上下文对齐方面面临重大挑战，在缺乏增强的上下文或符号集成的情况下存在明显局限。

Conclusion: CANDI-QA可作为推进上下文感知语言模型研究的关键基准，推动在高风险领域开发鲁棒、可信赖的AI系统。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in specialized domains like medical diagnostics and financial advisory necessitates evaluating capabilities beyond general knowledge. Traditional question-answering benchmarks often fail to capture the nuanced contextual grounding, user awareness, and domain understanding these fields require. To address this, we introduce CANDI-QA (Contextual Alignment for Niche Domains Question Answering), a novel dataset evaluating LLMs on delivering accurate, context-sensitive, and user-aligned answers in specialized settings. CANDI-QA features expert-curated question-answer pairs structured into two categories: (1) Information Assistance Questions, which are direct, factual queries requiring precise extraction, and (2) Applied Inference Questions, which are multi-hop reasoning tasks needing situational inference to generate actionable insights. We evaluate over ten diverse language models, from compact open-source to state-of-the-art proprietary systems. As a robust baseline, we present MTSS-Net, a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework combining neural retrieval with rule-based reasoning. Our findings highlight the profound challenges of achieving contextual alignment in niche domains, revealing the limitations of current LLMs without enhanced contextual or symbolic integration. Ultimately, CANDI-QA serves as a critical benchmark for advancing research in context-aware language models, stimulating the development of robust, trustworthy AI for high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [99] [G-SHARE: A Guideline-Based Structured Reasoning Framework for Human-Factor Event Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11892)
*Xingyu Xiao,Mao Du,Jiejuan Tong,Jingang Liang,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出G-SHARE框架，将CNNP九步人因事件诊断指南转化为多阶段推理流水线，包含证据提取、逐步诊断推理和一致性修复模块。在核工业真实数据集上，该方法显著优于一次性提示和传统机器学习基线，消融实验表明结构化推理和一致性约束对鲁棒诊断至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动或一次性大语言模型方法缺乏结构化推理、与正式诊断指南对齐有限，且可能生成逻辑不一致的结论，亟需一种能够遵循专家指南、具备可审计推理过程的智能诊断方法。

Method: G-SHARE框架：(1) 证据提取模块；(2) 逐步诊断推理（基于CNNP九步指南）；(3) 事后一致性修复模块。构建了核工业真实人因事件报告数据集及专家标注的金标准子集用于评估。

Result: G-SHARE在准确率和宏F1上显著优于一次性提示和传统机器学习基线；消融实验表明结构化推理和一致性强制执行是鲁棒诊断的关键，尤其在弱提示条件下效果更明显。

Conclusion: 将专家诊断指南转化为可审计的推理工作流具有重要价值，为安全关键行业中智能人因分析提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Human-factor event diagnosis is essential for learning from operational events in nuclear power plants, yet its quality depends strongly on expert interpretation of narrative reports and guideline-based reasoning.Existing data-driven or one-shot large language model approaches often lack structured reasoning, have limited alignment with formal diagnostic guidelines, and may generate logically inconsistent conclusions. To address this issue, this study proposes G-SHARE, a guideline-based structured reasoning framework that operationalizes the CNNP nine-step human-factor event diagnosis guideline into a multi-stage diagnostic pipeline.The framework consists of evidence extraction, stepwise diagnostic reasoning, and post-hoc consistency repair, enabling explicit use of report evidence, intermediate rationale generation, and logical validation of diagnostic outputs. A dataset of real human-factor event reports was constructed from Chinese nuclear industry sources, and a gold-standard subset annotated by domain experts was used for evaluation. Results show that G-SHARE substantially outperforms one-shot prompting and traditional machine learning baselines, with the strongest version achieving the best overall accuracy and macro-F1. Ablation results further indicate that structured reasoning and consistency enforcement are critical to robust diagnosis, especially under weak prompting conditions. The findings demonstrate the value of transforming expert diagnostic guidelines into auditable reasoning workflows, providing a practical pathway for intelligent human-factor analysis in safety-critical industries.

</details>


### [100] [I'm Sorry, but I Can't Help with Braille: Revealing Accessibility Failures in State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11893)
*Abdullah Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前大语言模型在韩语-盲文双向翻译中表现糟糕且不稳定，而小型模型T5-small通过监督微调在各项指标上取得显著且稳定的提升，揭示了大语言模型在结构受限的模态中的系统性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在结构受限、对可访问性至关重要的模态（如盲文）中的实际能力，验证多语言指令微调模型能否通过文本表示泛化到盲文翻译任务。

Method: 使用人工标注数据集评估多种大语言模型在韩语-盲文双向翻译上的零样本和提示学习表现，并与监督微调的小型模型（T5-small）进行对比，采用SacreBLEU、ChrF++、CER、BLEU、ROUGE-L、METEOR、CIDEr等标准指标。

Result: 大语言模型在所有指标上表现持续差且不稳定，与人类判断存在显著分歧；而T5-small通过监督微调在所有标准指标上取得大幅且稳定的提升。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型缺乏盲文感知的token化策略，且韩语与盲文模式间的对齐较弱，存在系统性局限；适度的任务特定监督微调是有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on many language tasks, but their capability in structurally constrained, accessibility-critical modalities such as Braille remains unclear. We evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs on bidirectional Korean-Braille translation using a human-annotated dataset. Despite expectations that multilingual, instruction-tuned models can generalize to Braille via text representations, we find consistently poor, unstable outputs and substantial disagreement with human judgments. These results point to missing Braille-aware tokenization and weak alignment between Korean and Braille patterns. In contrast, supervised fine-tuning of a small model (T5-small) on the same data yields large and stable gains over zero-shot and prompted LLM baselines across standard metrics (SacreBLEU, ChrF++, CER, BLEU, ROUGE-L, METEOR, CIDEr). Our findings reveal a systematic limitation of current LLMs and demonstrate the effectiveness of modest task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [101] [Graph-Based Detection of Disinformation Narrative Diffusion between Russian and Ukrainian Telegram Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11894)
*Yuliia Vistak,Viktoriia Makovska,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于图的框架，结合弱监督和传播图分析，在Telegram生态系统中检测和分析虚假信息叙事，通过将语义相关的主张聚类为叙事级别并建模跨渠道传播，实现可扩展的虚假信息叙事检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上虚假信息叙事的检测面临规模放大、快速演变和语言变异性等挑战，单靠帖子级别的分析难以捕捉协调性的叙事放大行为。

Method: 提出基于图的框架，结合弱监督（weak supervision）与传播图分析（propagation graph analysis），将语义相关的主张聚合为叙事级聚类，并建模其在互联渠道间的扩散过程。

Result: 结合文本信号与网络结构能够提供可扩展的虚假信息叙事检测方法，并能揭示虚假信息在大规模消息环境中的传播模式。

Conclusion: 整合文本信号与网络结构的图方法比单独的帖子级分析更有效地检测协调性虚假信息叙事，适用于大规模消息环境。

Abstract: Detecting disinformation narratives on social media is challenging due to the scale of amplification, rapid evolution, and linguistic variability of online content. We propose a graph-based framework for identifying and analyzing disinformation narratives in Telegram ecosystems by combining weak supervision with propagation graph analysis. The approach aggregates semantically related claims into narrative-level clusters and models their diffusion across interconnected channels. This enables the detection of coordinated narrative amplification that is difficult to capture through post-level analysis alone. Our results demonstrate that integrating textual signals with network structure provides a scalable method for detecting disinformation narratives and offers insights into how they propagate within large-scale messaging environments.

</details>


### [102] [TAKE: Trajectory-Aware Knowledge Estimation for Text Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11898)
*Tri-Nhan Vo,Dang Nguyen,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TAKE框架，通过影响函数和最优传输理论将文本数据集蒸馏至原大小的0.1%，同时保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文本语料库在存储、训练、微调和持续学习中的累积成本已成为现代NLP的瓶颈，亟需一种能在极端压缩率下保持任务保真度的数据蒸馏方法。

Method: 基于影响函数量化每个样本对下游目标的贡献，提出轨迹感知知识估计（TAKE）沿训练轨迹卷积知识影响得到单样本知识分数，再以该分数作为离散最优传输目标中的样本权重，从合成候选池中引导原型选择。

Result: 在文本分类和自然语言推理任务上，以0.1%压缩率（每类20个样本）实现高效数据利用，保持下游任务准确性。

Conclusion: TAKE在极端压缩下仍能保持任务保真度，具有理论依据，对核心集构建和数据中心AI有广泛启示。

Abstract: Large-scale text corpora have become a quiet bottleneck in modern NLP, not just in storage, but in the accumulated cost of training, fine-tuning, and continual learning. We propose a text dataset distillation framework that reduces corpora to as little as 0.1% of their original size while preserving downstream task fidelity. We approach distillation through the lens of influence functions, which quantify each sample's contribution to the downstream objective, a natural and principled basis for selection. We introduce Trajectory-Aware Knowledge Estimation (TAKE), which convolves the knowledge-based influence along the training trajectory into a single per-sample knowledge score, capturing informative samples. These scores serve as sample weights within a discrete Optimal Transport objective, guiding prototype selection from a synthetically generated candidate pool. We evaluate TAKE on downstream accuracy across text classification and natural language inference tasks at extreme compression (0.1% or 20 samples/class), showing that data efficiency is achievable without sacrificing task fidelity. The approach is theoretically grounded, with broader implications for coreset construction and data-centric AI. We release our source code at https://github.com/votrinhan88/take.

</details>


### [103] [Transforming LLMs into Efficient Cross-Encoders via Knowledge Distillation for RAG Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11933)
*Shreeya Dasa Lakshminath,Shubhan S*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过两阶段微调（监督微调+4bit量化），将LLaMA 3 (8B)改造为高效reranker，在RAG管道中替代传统交叉编码器，在降低推理开销的同时，答案相关性和正确性等指标提升14%-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉编码器在RAG中精度高但推理成本呈二次增长，限制了实时部署。需要一种既能保持高精度又能降低推理开销的reranker方案。

Method: 采用两阶段管道：1) 使用Unsloth框架和LoRA适配器在自定义查询-文档相关性数据集上进行监督微调LLaMA 3 (8B)；2) 4-bit量化实现高效推理。该模型替代双检索器RAG管道（BM25+密集向量检索）中的交叉编码器。

Result: 在领域特定QA基准上，相比交叉编码器基线：答案相关性提升14%，上下文精度提升16%，答案相似性提升19%，答案正确性提升21%，同时通过4-bit量化降低了推理开销。

Conclusion: 指令微调的大语言模型可以被改造为准确且高效的reranker，避免了传统交叉编码器的二次复杂度问题。

Abstract: Cross-encoders achieve high reranking accuracy in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines but impose quadratic inference costs that limit real-time deployment. We address this by fine-tuning LLaMA 3 (8B) as a drop-in reranker using a two-stage pipeline: supervised fine-tuning on a custom query-document relevance dataset via the Unsloth framework with LoRA adapters, followed by 4-bit quantization for efficient inference. The resulting model replaces the cross-encoder in a dual-retriever RAG pipeline combining BM25 and dense vector search. Evaluated on a domain-specific question-answering benchmark using the RAGAS framework, our fine-tuned LLaMA 3 reranker achieves gains of 14% in answer relevancy, 16% in context precision, 19% in answer similarity, and 21% in answer correctness over the cross-encoder baseline, while reducing inference overhead through 4-bit quantization. These results demonstrate that instruction-tuned LLMs can be adapted into accurate, efficient rerankers without the quadratic complexity of traditional cross-encoders.

</details>


### [104] [MAGE: Understanding Stability-Performance Trade-offs in Multi-component Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11944)
*Prateek Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过MAGE框架研究迭代提示优化中不同组件的交互作用，发现了"提示优化耦合效应"（POCE）：多个随机优化信号在封闭反思循环中相互作用时，会同时提升性能和放大方差。关键发现包括：失败驱动的反思至关重要；增加候选多样性会显著放大POCE信号；POCE存在headroom依赖性；在低数据场景下，精心设计的固定提示优于所有反思型优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究迭代提示优化中不同组件如何相互作用，以及它们组合时会产生怎样的效果，从而理解提示优化系统的底层机制。

Method: 提出MAGE（记忆增强目标导向提示进化）作为控制分析框架，集成情景记忆、多目标Pareto选择和自适应评估三个组件，通过受控消融实验研究组件交互，并在GSM8K-Hard等任务上使用gpt-4o-mini和Llama 3.1 8B进行验证。

Result: 1) 失败驱动的反思是必要的，仅依赖分数（OPRO）或抽象批评（Self-Refine）的方法无法改进提示；2) MAGE在GSM8K-Hard上达到46.4% vs GEPA的34.0%（+12.4%），方差可比（7.3% vs 7.0%）；3) 候选池从n=3扩大到n=5时，平均准确率提升21.6%但方差放大3.7倍；4) POCE具有headroom依赖性，基础模型准确率较高时方差放大消失；5) 低数据场景下（Ntrain=30），固定提示优于所有反思型优化器。

Conclusion: 提示优化系统表现为耦合随机过程，评估时应同时考虑性能和稳定性，而非仅关注峰值准确率。提示设计的选择（scaffold choice）主导了优化器本身的选择。

Abstract: How do different components of iterative prompt optimization interact, and what happens when they are combined? We investigate this through MAGE (Memory-Augmented Goal-directed Prompt Evolution), a controlled analysis framework for studying component interaction in prompt optimization. MAGE is not proposed as a superior optimizer in absolute terms; it integrates episodic memory, multi-objective Pareto selection, and adaptive evaluation as a platform for controlled ablation. Our experiments uncover a previously unreported phenomenon, the Prompt Optimization Coupling Effect (POCE): when multiple stochastic optimization signals operate within a closed reflective loop, they interact in ways that simultaneously improve performance and amplify variance, behavior that cannot be predicted by analyzing components in isolation. Three main findings emerge. First, failure-grounded reflection is essential: methods relying only on scores (OPRO) or abstract critique (Self-Refine) fail to improve prompts. Second, MAGE achieves 46.4% versus GEPA's 34.0% on GSM8K-Hard (+12.4%, P(MAGE>GEPA)=0.998, 5 seeds on gpt-4o-mini), with comparable variance (7.3% vs. 7.0%). Third, increasing candidate diversity reveals the clearest POCE signal: expanding the candidate pool from n=3 to n=5 improves mean accuracy by +21.6% while increasing variance by 3.7x. We further validate on Llama 3.1 8B and show POCE is headroom-dependent: when the base model already achieves high accuracy, variance amplification disappears. Finally, in low-data regimes (Ntrain=30), well-designed fixed prompts outperform all reflective optimizers, indicating that scaffold choice dominates optimizer choice. Our results suggest prompt optimization systems behave as coupled stochastic processes and should be evaluated in terms of both performance and stability, not just peak accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [Belief-reality separation lives in routing over a shared value slot in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11945)
*Oliver Steele,Jiangtao Wen,Yuxing Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型将信念与现实分离的计算机制：一个通用值槽绑定归因值，查询位置的路由器在“角色信念”和“现实”两个框架间切换。断言信念直接填充值槽，派生信念通过可见性门控回溯获取。解耦的路由子空间实现框架切换而不注入施予者的值。该行为在3B-7B参数间涌现，跨多种架构成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型如何在计算上实现信念（角色相信什么）与现实（真实情况）的分离机制，定位这一能力在模型计算中的具体位置和实现方式。

Method: 通过因果干预实验，定位值槽（绑定归因值）和路由器（选择读取框架）两个分离机制；区分断言信念（文本直接提供）和派生信念（需通过可见性推断）两条填充路径；训练子空间进行跨路径操控和框架切换干预。

Result: 信念-现实分离不在值槽本身（干预值槽对信念和现实读取影响相当），而存在于解耦的路由子空间中；派生路径依赖于可见性描述；跨三种架构、五种模型家族成立，行为在3B到7B参数间涌现。

Conclusion: 语言模型通过通用的“值槽-路由器”格式实现信念与现实分离，该机制同样适用于反事实、虚构、时间等非实际语境，揭示了模型处理多种非现实情境的统一计算原理。

Abstract: Capable language models hold what a character believes apart from what is true: told "Anna believes the cup is blue; in reality it is red," they answer blue about Anna and red about the world. Where in the computation does that separation live? We show it rests on two separable mechanisms at two positions. A generic value slot binds the attributed value. A router at the query position selects which frame, the character's belief or reality, a query reads out. Two routes fill the slot: an asserted belief, whose value the text supplies, binds in directly; a derived belief, whose value must be inferred from what the character could see, arrives by a visibility-gated lookback. A subspace trained on either route steers the other, and only the derived route depends on described visibility. The slot itself carries no belief-reality tag: intervening on it moves a reality readout as strongly as a belief one. The separation lives instead in a dissociated pair of routing subspaces, which flip a query between frames without injecting the donor's value. These results hold across three architectures, on stimuli de-confounded against theory-of-mind-benchmark shortcuts; the behavior itself emerges between 3B and 7B across five model families. This paper develops the single belief-reality axis in depth; a companion paper shows the same slot-and-router format is shared across the other non-actual contexts a sentence can open (counterfactual, fictional, temporal).

</details>


### [106] [Hybrid Continual Learning for Low-Resource Australian Aboriginal Language Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11946)
*Pravina Mylvaganam,Ting Dang,Eliathamby Ambikairajah,Vidhyasaharan Sethu,Jingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对极度数据稀缺的澳大利亚原住民语言识别问题，提出了两种混合持续学习方法（基于回放的弹性权重固化和约束引导知识蒸馏），在适应新语言的同时保持对高资源语言的性能，效果优于微调和现有持续学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将濒危澳大利亚原住民语言（AALs）整合到语音技术中面临极端数据稀缺问题，迁移学习虽有效但存在灾难性遗忘，持续学习在数据极少的情况下仍具挑战。

Method: 提出两种混合持续学习方法：Replay Augmented Elastic Weight Consolidation（基于回放的弹性权重固化）和Constraint Guided Knowledge Distillation（约束引导知识蒸馏），用于适配预训练语音模型进行AAL识别。

Result: 在Warlpiri、Dalabon和Dharawal三种语言上的实验表明，所提方法优于微调和现有持续学习基线，在适应多种AALs的同时保持了对先前学习的高资源语言的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的混合持续学习方法能有效缓解数据稀缺下迁移学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，为濒危语言识别提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Language identification is an important step toward integrating endangered Australian Aboriginal languages (AALs) into speech technologies supporting language revitalisation and digital inclusion. However, extreme data scarcity limits model performance. Transfer learning from high-resource languages shows promise but often suffers from catastrophic forgetting when adapting to new languages. Continual learning (CL) can mitigate this issue, though it remains challenging with very limited data. To address this, we propose two hybrid continual learning methods: Replay Augmented Elastic Weight Consolidation and Constraint Guided Knowledge Distillation to adapt pretrained speech models for AAL identification while preserving previously learned knowledge. Experiments on Warlpiri, Dalabon and Dharawal show that the proposed methods outperform fine-tuning and existing CL baselines, improving adaptation to multiple AALs while maintaining performance on previously learnt high-resource languages.

</details>


### [107] [Evaluating Nonuniform Dependability Across Response Conditions: A Conditional Generalizability Framework Illustrated in Automated Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11981)
*Yi Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出条件概化框架，将自动评分配置视为测量条件全域，通过熵定义的反应层条件化证据，诊断非均匀的信赖度。在二语写作自动评分中展示：聚合信赖度约0.76，熵分层后呈现梯度下降（0.88→0.84），表明不同层需要不同的决策研究设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合可靠性估计会掩盖不同反应条件下测量设计负担的异质性，单一G研究或D研究可能错误描述特定层设计是否充分。现有概化理论扩展主要处理AI生成的项目变体（反应侧问题），但缺乏对AI中介评分配置（评分侧问题）的对应处理方法。

Method: 提出条件概化框架，包含三个组成部分：（1）将自动评分配置（编码器架构和评分头家族）视为可容许测量条件的全域；（2）将分析性D研究投影与经验性配置扫描对比，得到两个设计充分性估计量；（3）基于熵定义的反应层条件化证据，将熵作为操作性的分层变量而非写作质量的结构性主张。

Result: 在限时二语写作自动评分中，聚合信赖度Phi≈0.76；熵分层后信赖度保持较高但呈现稳健下降梯度（Phi=0.88, 0.87, 0.84），表明最高熵层需要最多的交叉条件。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可移植的工作流程，用于评估非均匀的信赖度，弥补了现有概化理论在评分侧AI配置问题上的空白。

Abstract: Aggregate reliability estimates can obscure heterogeneity in measurement-design burden across response conditions, so a single G- or D-study may mischaracterize a design's adequacy for particular strata. This study introduces a conditional generalizability framework with three components. First, automated scoring configurations -- the encoder architectures and scoring-head families admissible within a fixed pipeline -- are treated as a universe of admissible measurement conditions rather than incidental modeling choices. Second, analytical D-study projections are compared with empirical configuration sweeps over a finite scoring pool, yielding two estimands of design adequacy whose agreement or divergence diagnoses the realized configuration universe. Third, evidence is conditioned on entropy-defined response strata, treating entropy as an operational stratification variable, not a construct claim about writing quality. Whereas recent generalizability-theory extensions address AI-generated item variants on the response side, this framework addresses the analogous scoring-side problem: AI-mediated scoring configurations. Demonstrated with automated essay scoring of timed L2 writing, the realized design was dependable in aggregate (Phi approx 0.76). Re-estimated within entropy strata, dependability stayed high but declined modestly and robustly (Phi = 0.88, 0.87, 0.84) -- a gradient implying different decision-study requirements, the highest-entropy stratum requiring the most crossed conditions. The framework offers a portable workflow for evaluating nonuniform dependability.

</details>


### [108] [Agentic systems for breast cancer treatment recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12051)
*Vinicius Anjos de Almeida,Nícolas Henrique Borges,Leonardo Vicenzi,Helena Kociolek,Sarah Miriã de Castro Rocha,Frederico Nassif Gomes,Júlia Cristina Ferreira Ribeiro,Lucas Emanuel Silva e Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种agentic LLM系统在乳腺癌治疗推荐生成中的表现，最佳系统（Claude Opus 4.8 + D&C+SA pipeline）得分为0.594 ± 0.025。工具使用和自主性提升效果不一，存在推荐错误、理由不充分、引用错误、信息过时和过度自信等临床相关问题，表明AI系统尚不足以用于无监督临床实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在临床决策支持中的潜力日益受到关注，但在复杂的肿瘤治疗规划场景中，其可靠性仍不明确。本文旨在系统评估agentic LLM系统在乳腺癌治疗推荐任务中的实际表现与局限性。

Method: 使用72例真实临床病例（涵盖I-IV期乳腺癌）和1,147个病例特定评估标准（通过AIRG方法生成），比较了7种pipeline，包括单LLM基线、工具增强系统和多智能体架构（具备事实核查和自主子智能体生成能力）。评估标准生成者可访问真实临床决策，而被评估模型无此信息。

Result: 最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.8 + D&C+SA pipeline）得分为0.594 ± 0.025。工具使用和智能体自主性提升效果不一，部分场景提升、其他场景下降。表现因临床领域和疾病分期而异。肿瘤专家错误分析揭示了持续的临床相关失败，包括推荐错误或遗漏、理由不充分、引用错误、信息过时和过度自信。

Conclusion: Agentic LLM系统能够生成临床相关的乳腺癌推荐，但在无监督临床使用中仍存在不足，可靠性不足以支持直接临床应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for clinical decision support, but their reliability in complex oncology treatment planning remains unclear. We evaluated agentic LLM systems for breast cancer treatment recommendation generation using 72 real clinical cases across stages I to IV and 1,147 case-specific rubrics generated through Asymmetric Information Rubric Generation (AIRG), in which the rubric generator had access to real clinical decisions unavailable to the evaluated models. Seven pipelines were compared, including single-LLM baselines, tool-augmented systems, and multi-agent architectures with fact checking and autonomous subagent spawning. The best-performing configuration, Claude Opus 4.8 with the D&C+SA pipeline, achieved a global score of 0.594 $\pm$ 0.025. Tool use and increased agent autonomy had mixed effects, improving performance in some settings but degrading it in others. Performance varied by clinical domain and disease stage, and oncologist-led error analysis revealed persistent clinically relevant failures, including incorrect or missing recommendations, flawed justifications, citation errors, outdated claims, and overconfidence. These findings suggest that agentic LLM systems can generate clinically relevant breast cancer recommendations, but remain insufficient for unsupervised clinical use.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Parallel Tracking: Interactive Multi-Feature Fusion Drives Semantic Reconstruction from Non-invasive Brain Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12071)
*Boda Xiao,Xiran Xu,Songyi Li,Yujie Yan,Xihong Wu,Heping Cheng,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多特征融合框架，通过非线性交叉注意力机制整合静态词向量（W2V）和动态上下文表示（GPT），在非侵入性脑电信号语义重建任务中取得了最优性能，揭示了大脑同时利用稳定词义与动态语境进行语言理解的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义解码器仅依赖静态词汇表示或动态上下文表示，忽视了人脑整合稳定词属性和动态语境的能力，导致跨模态对齐严重的信息损失。

Method: 提出多特征融合框架，包含线性朴素拼接（Naive Concatenation）和非线性多头交叉注意力（Multi-Head Cross-Attention）两种集成方式，通过交互式门控机制协同处理静态（W2V）和动态（GPT）语义特征。

Result: 性能排序为 Cross-Att > Concat > GPT > W2V，非线性交叉注意力融合方法达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 神经语言解码受益于模拟大脑对上下文信息和核心词属性的协同调制，而非依赖孤立的单一特征，为无创脑到文本解码提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous semantic reconstruction from non-invasive neural recordings remains limited by the representational mismatch between semantic feature spaces and neural coding patterns, which severely impedes cross-modal alignment between high-noise neural signals and target semantic features. Prior semantic decoders have predominantly relied on static lexical representations or dynamic contextualized representations in isolation. This single-dimension approach inevitably leads to severe information loss, as it fails to account for the human brain's capacity to integrate stable word attributes and dynamic contexts simultaneously.To bridge this gap, this study introduces a multi-feature fusion framework for non-invasive semantic reconstruction, systematically benchmarking two integration approaches: linear Naive Concatenation and non-linear Multi-Head Cross-Attention. Within this framework, our approach complements static lexical representations (W2V) with dynamic contextual representations (GPT) via an interactive gating mechanism to facilitate cooperative processing during language comprehension.Evaluated through extensive semantic reconstruction and text generation experiments, our framework reveals a robust performance hierarchy: Cross-Att > Concat > GPT > W2V. Crucially, the non-linear cross-attention fusion method achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating that neural language decoding benefits from simulating the collaborative modulation between contextual information and core lexical attributes rather than depending on isolated individual features, while also offering a viable non-invasive brain-to-text decoding method.

</details>


### [110] [The Capacity of Thought: Benchmarking Llama 3.2 in Semantic fMRI Neural Language Decoding and Improving the Huth Encoding-Model Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12079)
*Milos Suvakovic,Dom Marhoefer,Glenn Grant-Richards,Aidan Pinero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两种方法研究fMRI信号连续语言解码：改进的岭回归编码管线（METEOR=0.149）和fMRIFlamingo模型（Top-1准确率42.86%）。但盲控实验表明后者解码成功主要源于语言先验而非神经信号，揭示高容量语言模型可能掩盖解码失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从fMRI信号中解码连续语言是非侵入式脑机接口的核心挑战，现有方法性能有限，且高容量语言模型在解码中的真实贡献尚未被严格评估。

Method: 1) 改进Huth等人的岭回归编码管线：扩展体素选择（10K→15K）、用GPT-2 medium替换GPT-1作为波束搜索提议模型、GPU加速bootstrap训练。2) fMRIFlamingo：将BOLD活动通过可学习的脑分词器和Perceiver Resampler映射到冻结的Llama-3.2-1B，并加入可训练的门控交叉注意力层。

Result: 改进的岭回归管线在UTS03受试者上达到METEOR=0.149、BLEU-1=0.200，相比复制基线提升11%。fMRIFlamingo在1-in-100排序任务中达到42.86% Top-1准确率，但零输入盲控消融实验得到几乎相同的分数。

Conclusion: 高容量语言模型并不天然提升fMRI解码性能，且可能在没有严格盲控评估的情况下掩盖解码失败；fMRI解码研究必须纳入盲控实验来验证神经信号的真实贡献。

Abstract: Decoding continuous language from fMRI signals remains a core challenge in non-invasive brain-computer interface research. We present two complementary investigations. First, we improve the Huth et al. ridge regression encoding pipeline through expanded voxel selection (10K->15K), substitution of GPT-2 medium for GPT-1 as the beam-search proposal model, and GPU-accelerated bootstrap training, achieving mean METEOR = 0.149 and BLEU-1 = 0.200 across three held-out narratives for subject UTS03 -- an 11% relative METEOR gain over our replication baseline. Second, we introduce fMRIFlamingo, which maps BOLD activity to a frozen Llama-3.2-1B with trainable gated cross-attention layers via a learned brain tokenizer and a Perceiver Resampler. Despite achieving 42.86% Top-1 accuracy on a 1-in-100 ranking task, well above chance, a blind control ablation with zeroed fMRI inputs yields near-identical scores, revealing that apparent decoding success is driven primarily by the frozen language prior rather than by neural input. These results demonstrate that high-capacity language models do not inherently improve fMRI decoding and can actively obscure failures without rigorous blind-control evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [CityBehavEx: A Scalable and Empirically Validated LLM-Assisted Urban Simulation Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12086)
*Gustavo H. Santos,Aline Viana,Thiago H Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CityBehavEx是一个交互式LLM辅助城市模拟平台，通过结合传统人类移动模型与微调跨编码器，实现了10万智能体75天模拟在一小时内完成，并生成更符合真实世界时空语义分布的移动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体城市模拟器能生成语义丰富的城市日常活动，但存在两个问题：一是难以规模化扩展（成本高），二是缺乏对经验移动模式的有效验证。

Method: CityBehavEx不依赖LLM为每个智能体行为进行推理，而是将成熟的人类移动模型与微调跨编码器相结合，用于评估智能体档案、日程安排和活动转换之间的语义对齐。

Result: 在单张消费级GPU上，不到一小时即可完成10万智能体75天的模拟。生成的移动模式在空间、时间和语义分布上更贴近真实世界数据。

Conclusion: CityBehavEx为大规模城市模拟提供了一种高效且可验证的解决方案，兼顾了语义丰富性和经验可验证性。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based multi-agent urban simulators can generate semantically rich city routines, but they remain costly to scale and are often weakly validated against empirical mobility patterns. We present CityBehavEx, an interactive LLM-assisted urban simulation platform that scales to city-size populations, exposes agent behavior for inspection, supports empirical validation, and generates mobility patterns that better match real-world spatial, temporal, and semantic distributions. Instead of invoking large language models for every agent action, CityBehavEx combines established human mobility models with fine-tuned cross-encoders that estimate semantic alignment between agent profiles, schedules, and activity transitions. This design enables large-scale simulations, as demonstrated in a case study of 100,000 agents over 75 days in under one hour on a single consumer GPU. The platform allows users to define simulation regions, launch experiments, inspect trajectories and activity traces, debug unrealistic behaviors, and validate generated routines against real-world mobility, time-use, and semantic metrics.

</details>


### [112] [Token Reduction Is Not Cost Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12161)
*Sarel Weinberger,Amir Hozez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（2,848次分析运行）质疑了API编码智能体中上下文缩减技术的评估标准。研究发现：1）提示缓存流量占总成本约80-87%；2）工具输出缩减不能可靠预测实际计费成本下降（38%的token减少反而导致6.8%的成本增加）；3）压缩会通过破坏关键证据（如编辑锚点）损害任务完成率（从27/40降至15/40）。研究提出应以"成功调整后的计费成本"作为评估标准，而非单纯token缩减量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文缩减层（命令输出压缩器、检索排序器、负载优化代理）通常仅以文本移除量作为评估指标，忽略了这些技术在降低实际计费成本、保持任务成功率和不延长执行轨迹方面的真实表现。论文旨在建立更全面的评估框架。

Method: 采用预指定、哈希冻结的配对实验设计，进行2,908次Claude Code运行（分析2,848次），覆盖103个任务、7个代码仓库和3个模型。比较基线方法与两代基于钩子的压缩方法和API边界代理，总执行量约5,500次计费运行。通过重建四组件成本结构分析实际账单数据。

Result: 1）提示缓存流量占主导：缓存创建和读取占总成本约87%（实际账单约80%），8.7%的美元加权残差无法归因；2）工具输出缩减与实际成本降低无可靠关联：减少38%原始token的组别反而增加6.8%成本（95%CI: +2.8%至+11.3%）；3）压缩损害任务完成：在SWE-bench衍生Go任务中，压缩将补丁应用率从27/40降至15/40，压缩版本文本化方案以更高单次求解成本获得更少求解。

Conclusion: 提出分层证据标准，应以"成功调整后的计费成本"（success-adjusted billed cost）而非单纯token缩减量作为评估上下文缩减技术的核心指标。单纯追求token减少可能导致实际成本上升和任务成功率下降，需要在成本优化与任务效能之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Context-reduction layers for API-based coding agents, including command-output compressors, retrieval rankers, and payload-optimizing proxies, are usually evaluated by how much text they remove. We ask instead: when does reducing retrieved context or tool output lower the actual billed cost of a coding agent without reducing task success or lengthening its trajectory?
  Our primary evidence is a pre-specified, hash-frozen, paired campaign of 2,908 provider-billed Claude Code runs, of which 2,848 were analyzed, covering 103 tasks, seven repositories, and three models. The campaign compared a baseline with two generations of hook-based compression and an API-boundary proxy, within a broader measured program of roughly 5,500 billed executions.
  Three findings emerge. First, prompt-cache traffic dominated cost composition. Cache creation and reads accounted for about 87% of reconstructed four-component cost, or about 80% of the actual bill, with an 8.7% dollar-weighted residual that retained telemetry could not attribute. On Haiku 4.5, this residual scaled with thinking effort.
  Second, tool-output reduction did not reliably predict billed-cost reduction. An arm that removed 38% of estimated raw tool-output tokens had 6.8% higher paired cost (95% CI: +2.8% to +11.3%), while per-task reduction was only weakly associated with cost change (Pearson r = 0.15, CI crossing zero).
  Third, compression can harm task completion by removing action-critical evidence. In a small single-shot study on SWE-bench-derived Go tasks, compression reduced patch application from 27/40 to 15/40 by corrupting verbatim edit anchors, and the compressed grounded arm produced fewer solves at higher observed cost per solve.
  We propose a layered evidence standard centered on success-adjusted billed cost rather than token reduction alone.

</details>


### [113] [We Hebben Een Serieus Translatie: Modeling Intercomprehension as Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12169)
*Thomas Hikaru Clark,Edward Gibson,Roger Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于贝叶斯框架的噪声信道模型来解释跨语言互通理解（intercomprehension）现象，该模型仅使用L1的语言模型来对L2话语的潜在翻译进行评分，并通过通用噪声模型基于形式相似性或符号规则推断L2与L1词汇间的映射。人类行为实验表明，该模型比消融版本和更大模型的零样本提示更接近人类表现，为跨语言理解提供了认知上合理的计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么说话者能够部分理解与其母语相关的陌生语言（零样本跨语言理解），即探索互通理解（intercomprehension）背后的认知机制。

Method: 在贝叶斯框架下扩展噪声信道推理模型，使用L1语言模型对L2话语的潜在翻译假设进行评分，并结合通用噪声模型（基于形式相似性或符号规则）推断L2与L1词汇的映射关系。通过英语-荷兰语、西班牙语-意大利语、俄语-乌克兰语三组语言对进行人类行为实验验证。

Result: 完整模型比消融版本和更大规模模型的零样本提示更贴近人类互通理解的表现分布。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个认知上合理的互通理解计算模型，凸显了理解者在真实跨语言场景中面对广泛不确定性时能够进行灵活推理的能力。

Abstract: Intercomprehension refers to partial intelligibility of an unfamiliar language (L2) by a speaker of a related language (L1). How is this zero-shot cross-language comprehension possible? In this work, we extend past work on algorithmic models of noisy-channel inference to model intercomprehension in a Bayesian framework. The model uses an LM in L1 only for scoring latent hypotheses about the translations of observed L2 utterances, and a general-purpose noise model to infer a mapping between L2 and L1 words based on either form-based similarity or symbolic rules. We then conduct a human behavioral experiment, eliciting inferences for utterances in Dutch, Italian, and Ukrainian from speakers of English, Spanish, and Russian, respectively. Our full model shows a closer alignment to the distribution of human intercomprehension performance than ablations, and also compares favorably to zero-shot prompting of much larger models. These results provide a cognitively plausible computational model of intercomprehension, and highlight the flexible inferences made by comprehenders under wide uncertainty in real-world cross-language scenarios. We share our code publicly.

</details>


### [114] [Entropy in Semantic Memory Navigation in Blind and Sighted Individuals: The Effect of Visual Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12185)
*Felipe D. Toro-Hernández,Rodrigo Lagos,Sergio E. Chaigneau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过属性列举任务比较盲人和视力正常者在语义记忆导航中的差异，发现视力正常者对抽象概念的语义熵更高，而盲人对视觉显著性具体概念的语义熵更高，表明视觉经验在语义记忆组织和动态导航中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验语义记忆的具身化理论，探究概念知识是否需要视觉经验作为基础，通过先天盲人群体作为关键测试对象。

Method: 使用属性列举任务收集盲人和视力正常者对具体和抽象概念的反应，采用基于词嵌入的自然语言处理指标——语义熵来量化检索的可预测性，通过广义线性混合模型分析组间差异。

Result: 视力正常者表现出抽象概念比具体概念更高的语义熵；盲人群体则没有这种抽象-具体差异，反而对视觉显著性高的具体概念（如企鹅）表现出更高的语义熵。

Conclusion: 视觉经验在语义记忆的组织和动态导航中扮演重要角色，缺乏视觉经验会导致语义记忆的组织模式发生系统性偏移。

Abstract: Embodied accounts of semantic memory highlight the role of sensorimotor systems in acquiring and storing knowledge. Congenitally blind populations offer a critical test bed for these assumptions, providing an opportunity to assess whether conceptual grounding requires visual experience. In this study, we assessed semantic memory navigation differences between blind and sighted individuals using a property listing task with concrete and abstract concepts. We computed semantic entropy, an embedding-based natural language processing metric that captures the predictability of retrieval. Generalized linear mixed models revealed distinct navigation patterns across groups: while sighted individuals showed higher entropy for abstract than concrete concepts, blind participants did not. Instead, blind individuals exhibited higher entropy for visually salient concrete concepts (e.g., penguin). These results underscore the role of visual experience in the organization and dynamic navigation of semantic memory.

</details>


### [115] [Comparing Semantic Navigation in Humans and Large Language Models using Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12195)
*Gabriel Paris-Colombo,Rodrigo M. Cabral-Carvalho,Felipe D. Toro-Hernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 人类在语义搜索中表现出更高的熵、更大的语义步长和更广的分布范围，而大语言模型（GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Pro, Claude-Sonnet-4.5）即使调整温度参数也无法完全复现人类的搜索模式，表明当前模型架构未能捕捉人类在局部利用与全局探索之间的独特平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类与大型语言模型在语义记忆检索（概念空间导航）中的搜索动态差异，验证LLM是否能复现人类语义搜索行为。

Method: 收集82名人类被试的词语流畅性任务数据及三种LLM（GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Pro, Claude-Sonnet-4.5）在8种温度设置下的输出，应用基于轨迹的NLP指标量化三个维度：熵（步长可预测性）、到下一项的距离（连续语义步长）、到质心的距离（全局分散程度）。

Result: 人类在所有三个维度（熵、语义步长、全局分散度）上均高于所有LLM。温度调谐仅能实现部分对齐——个别指标在特定温度设置下能与人类匹配，但没有任何配置能在所有维度上复现人类的完整剖面。

Conclusion: 人类语义搜索实现了局部利用与全局探索之间的独特平衡，当前大语言模型架构未能成功复现这一特征。

Abstract: Semantic memory retrieval can be conceptualized as navigation through conceptual space. We compared semantic search dynamics between humans and three large language models (GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Pro, Claude-Sonnet-4.5) using verbal fluency data. By applying trajectory-based NLP metrics to the items generated by 82 human participants and LLM output across eight temperature settings, we quantified three complementary dimensions: entropy (step size predictability), distance to next (successive semantic steps), and distance to centroid (global dispersion). Humans exhibited higher entropy, larger semantic steps and broader dispersion than all LLMs, indicating more variable and exploratory search. Temperature tuning produced only partial alignments, as individual metrics matched between humans and LLMs at specific settings, but no configuration reproduced the complete human profile (in all dimensions). These findings suggest that human semantic search implements a distinctive balance between local exploitation and global exploration that current model architectures fail to reproduce.

</details>


### [116] [Fine-Tuned Multi-Agent Framework for Detecting OCEAN in Life Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12215)
*Rasiq Hussain,Darshil Italiya,Joshua Oltmanns,Mehak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于微调的多智能体框架，通过掩码语言建模和心理测量监督，让子智能体从高、中、低不同视角评估每个OCEAN人格特质，再由裁判LLM聚合判断，从而更准确、可解释地从文本中推断人格特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从文本评估人格面临特质隐式、上下文依赖、长叙述中表达微妙等挑战；LLMs虽能处理长文本但存在预训练带来的"类人格"偏差，单模型推断结果不一致。

Method: 微调多智能体框架：对每个OCEAN特质，子智能体分别被条件化为高、低、中立三种视角，通过MLM和心理测量监督进行训练；裁判LLM聚合各子智能体输出，进行综合比较后生成最终特质预测。

Result: 在生活叙事数据集上通过定量和定性实验（包括基线对比、消融实验和推理质量分析）验证了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可扩展且可解释的基于文本的人格推断方案，凸显了以心理测量监督为基础的多智能体推理的优势。

Abstract: Accurately assessing personality from text is challenging because traits are latent, context-dependent, and often subtly expressed across long narratives. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities by processing extensive textual contexts, but pretraining of these models can induce latent "personality-like" biases, making single-model inferences inconsistent. We propose a fine-tuned multi-agent framework for detecting OCEAN personality traits, in which sub-agents are conditioned to adopt high, low, or neutral perspectives for each trait through masked language modeling (MLM) and psychometric supervision. A judge LLM aggregates and compares sub-agent outputs to generate final trait predictions, capturing multiple complementary perspectives while mitigating individual model biases. We evaluate the framework on life narrative dataset through quantitative and qualitative experiments, including baselines, ablations, and inference quality analyses. Our approach offers a scalable and interpretable method for text-based personality inference, highlighting the benefits of multi-agent reasoning grounded in psychometric supervision.

</details>


### [117] [RCWT: Measuring Task-Budget Displacement from Coordination Content in LLM Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12216)
*Brenda Lelis,Rodrigo Cabral-Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出回合制上下文窗口测试（RCWT），用于测量多智能体和记忆增强型LLM系统中协调内容对任务预算的挤占效应。在固定上下文预算下，协调内容增加会显著降低任务表现，但当任务证据保持完整时，即使协调比例高达95%，模型仍能正确完成任务。这表明RCWT是一种上下文分配预算的测量工具，而非证明协调内容本身会导致语义干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体和记忆增强型LLM系统常将协调内容、共享状态、历史讨论、工具输出、摘要和角色指令等全部塞入同一提示中，导致每个用于协调的token都会挤占任务指令或证据的空间。这种固定上下文预算下的分配问题尚缺乏系统的测量方法。

Method: 提出RCWT（Roundtable Context Window Test）协议，在控制总预算、位置顺序、任务族和评分标准的同时，变化协调内容的数量进行测量。主要实验在W=4096上下文窗口下进行上下文依赖回忆任务，并设置了完整任务消融实验（保持任务/参考块完整，通过扩展总提示长度增加协调token）。

Result: 在主要任务中，三个商业模型在中等开销下表现接近基线，当剩余参考证据降至几百token时性能急剧下降。窗口缩放摘要符合任务特定残差预算解释。在完整任务消融中，所有模型在协调比例高达95%时仍能正确返回每个评分字段。

Conclusion: RCWT主实验中的性能悬崖应解读为任务预算被挤占（task-budget displacement），而非协调内容本身导致语义干扰。RCWT是一种上下文分配预算的测量原语，而非完整的多智能体收益或会话级协调理论。

Abstract: Multi-agent and memory-augmented LLM systems often place coordination content, shared state, prior discussion, tool outputs, summaries, and role instructions, inside the same finite prompt used for the current task. This creates a practical allocation problem: every token spent on coordination is unavailable to task instructions or evidence when a call is assembled under a fixed context budget. We introduce the Roundtable Context Window Test (RCWT), a controlled protocol for measuring this task-budget displacement effect. RCWT varies coordination content while controlling total budget, position order, task family, and scoring. In the main context-dependent recall task at $W=4096$, three commercial models remain near baseline through moderate overhead and then degrade sharply once residual reference evidence falls to a few hundred tokens. Window-scaling summaries are consistent with a task-specific residual-budget interpretation rather than a fixed percentage threshold, but we treat this as descriptive evidence rather than a universal law. To test whether the fixed-budget cliff persists when task evidence remains intact, we add an intact-task ablation: the full task/reference block is kept present while coordination tokens increase by expanding total prompt length. In that setting, all tested calls return every scored field correctly across GPT-4.1-mini, Claude Haiku 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Flash up to a 95\% coordination ratio. This ablation narrows the claim: the main RCWT cliff is best read as task-budget displacement, not as proof that coordination volume alone causes semantic interference in the original open-ended task. RCWT is therefore a measurement primitive for context-allocation budgeting, not a complete theory of multi-agent benefit or session-level coordination.

</details>


### [118] [Fin-Analyst at FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: A Live Hybrid Trading Agent with LLM Specialists and Rule-Based Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12233)
*Mohotarema Rashid,Lingzi Hong,Junhua Ding,K. S. M. Tozammel Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Fin-Analyst是一个混合智能体系统，在FinMMEval 2026 Task 3中排名第一：使用八专家LLM流水线处理TSLA股票实现+13.51%收益（Sharpe 4.10，胜率88%），BTC采用轻量规则投票，并揭示了无记忆代理的缺陷及事件驱动8-K披露的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易智能体主要聚焦于美国股票且缺乏真实部署证据，论文旨在构建一个混合智能体系统验证其在真实市场中的有效性。

Method: 针对TSLA采用八专长LLM流水线（新闻、SEC文件、基本面、分析师预测、技术指标、社交情绪等）由元智能体聚合；BTC采用轻量级基于规则的三个信号投票机制。

Result: TSLA最终排名第一，实现+13.51%收益，超过买入持有28.33个百分点（Sharpe 4.10，胜率88%）；BTC策略持平但高于大幅下跌的基线。

Conclusion: 消融实验发现8-K事件驱动披露是最重要的TSLA信号；误差分析表明无记忆智能体会重复错误，固定阈值BTC规则在横盘市场中因噪声交易亏损，因此需要记忆感知的LLM解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents show promising performance in equity markets, yet remain narrowly focused on US equities with little evidence from live deployment. We present Fin-Analyst, a hybrid agent for FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: an eight-specialist LLM pipeline over news, SEC filings, fundamentals, analyst forecasts, technical indicators, and social sentiment, aggregated by a Meta-Agent for Tesla (TSLA), and a lightweight rule based three-signal vote for Bitcoin (BTC). On the final official leaderboard (accessed 2026-07-05), Fin-Analyst ranks first of all agents on TSLA with a +13.51% return, +28.33 points over Buy-and-Hold (Sharpe 4.10, 88% win rate), while the BTC vote ends flat yet well above a sharply falling baseline. Relative to the interim performance, the asset ranking reversed, indicating that short live windows yield volatility-sensitive rankings. Ablation identifies event-driven 8-K disclosures as the most influential TSLA signal. Error analysis shows that the memoryless agents repeat wrong calls for days at a time, and that the fixed-threshold BTC rules lost money by trading on noise in a sideways market while the LLM pipeline gained under similar conditions, motivating a memory-aware, LLM-based successor for both assets.

</details>


### [119] [FinResearchBench II: A Deep Research Benchmark with Consensus-Derived Gold Rubrics for Distinguishing Financial Report Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12252)
*Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Sinuo Wang,Yan Gu,Chao Li,Zhenliang Xiong,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人类专家参与的可扩展流水线，用于自动生成高质量评估标准（rubrics）并构建金融深度研究基准，最终通过共识衍生黄金标准对10个深度研究系统进行排序，系统通过率从58.58%到22.23%不等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体在生成金融长文本报告方面日益普及，但大规模评估受限于需要人类专家定义和执行高质量评估标准，存在严重的效率瓶颈。

Method: 从104个真实用户查询构建金融深度研究基准，自动合成14,450个查询特定候选rubrics；通过一致性过滤器（三个LLM评委对同一查询下所有报告一致同意）和可区分性过滤器（rubric至少分配一个多数"是"和一个多数"否"标签）获得共识衍生黄金标准。

Result: LLM评估与人类评估在标签级别一致性达98.67%；保留3,687个通过一致性过滤的rubrics，其中2,600个具有可区分性；10个深度研究系统的通过率范围为58.58%到22.23%，呈现清晰差异化排名。

Conclusion: 该流水线移除了人类专家在rubric生成和执行中的参与，具有天然的可扩展性，适用于基准评估、自动系统比较以及评估驱动的系统改进研究。

Abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly used to produce long-form financial reports, yet large-scale evaluation remains bottlenecked by the need for human experts to define and execute high-quality rubrics. We address this problem by proposing a scalable pipeline for generating high-quality rubrics without human experts in the final loop. We build a financial deep research benchmark from 104 real-world user queries and automatically synthesize 14,450 query-specific candidate rubrics from model-generated reports. To justify removing human experts from rubric execution, we compare rubric judgments from three human experts with those from a three-LLM judge panel on a sampled subset, and show that LLM-based evaluation is sufficiently consistent with human evaluation to replace it for large-scale rubric screening, including 98.67\% label-level agreement on jointly unanimous items. We then derive consensus-derived gold rubrics through two filters: a strict consistency filter, which keeps a rubric only if the three LLM judges unanimously agree on every report under the same query, and a distinguishability filter, which keeps a rubric only if it assigns at least one majority-yes and at least one majority-no label across the evaluated systems. This process retains 3,687 consistency-passed rubrics, of which 2,600 remain distinguishable and form the final set of consensus-derived gold rubrics. Using this final rubric set, we obtain clearly differentiated rankings across 10 deep research systems, with item-level pass rates ranging from 58.58\% to 22.23\%. More broadly, because the pipeline removes human-expert execution from rubric generation and evaluation, it is naturally scalable for benchmark evaluation, automatic system comparison, and future studies of evaluation-driven system improvement.

</details>


### [120] [A Shared Subcircuit Lets LLMs Count Down Across Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12279)
*Jacob Dunefsky,Wes Gurnee,Emmanuel Ameisen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct模型中识别了一个通用的"倒计时子电路"机制，用于跟踪剩余token数并执行需要计数能力的任务，并发现该机制在不同模型和任务中共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能够执行需要跟踪剩余token数的任务（如写固定长度句子、在正确密码子结束DNA序列、格式化ASCII表格），但背后的机制尚不清楚。本文旨在逆向工程揭示这一通用机制的内部工作原理。

Method: 首先在控制环境中（固定长度句子以指定单词结尾）隔离倒计时子电路；然后研究子电路使用的表示几何结构，发现与之前在前沿LLM中识别出的相同基序；最后通过无监督探针在自然语言数据集中发现该子电路被用于多种其他需要推断目标长度的任务。

Result: 成功在Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct中定位了一个通用的倒计时子电路，该电路比较当前位置与目标长度并估算剩余时间。该子电路使用的基序与之前在其他模型上发现的相同，表明该机制跨模型共享。该子电路也被用于需要从上下文推断目标长度的自然语言任务。

Conclusion: 逆向工程子电路有助于理解行为如何从单个示例泛化到多种不同任务甚至不同模型，揭示了语言模型中计数相关能力的通用机制。

Abstract: Writing a sentence of exactly twelve words; ending a DNA sequence at the right codon; formatting an ASCII table. These are all tasks that language models can do that requires tracking how many tokens remain before a target. In this work, we identify in Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct a general mechanism for performing these tasks: a "countdown subcircuit" that compares the current position to a goal length and estimates the time remaining until then. We first isolate a countdown subcircuit in a controlled setting, in which the model is tasked with writing a fixed-length sentence ending in a specified word. We then investigate the geometry of the representations used by the subcircuit, and find that the subcircuit uses an identical motif previously identified in a frontier LLM on a separate task, thus suggesting that this motif is shared across models. Finally, we use unsupervised probing on a natural language dataset to find a variety of other tasks where this subcircuit is used, including tasks where the goal length is inferred from context rather than explicitly stated. Our work suggests that reverse-engineering subcircuits allows us to understand how behaviors generalize from a single example to many different tasks and even models.

</details>


### [121] [LakeQuest: A Three-Domain Benchmark for Grounded Question Answering across Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12310)
*Michael Solodko,Steven Gong,Guangwei Yu,Satya Krishna Gorti,Jesse C. Cresswell,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LakeQuest是一个人工验证的基准测试，包含9,846个问答对，用于评估真实数据湖环境下的端到端检索-合成流水线。覆盖AI/ML元数据、零售银行和多模态生物医学药物信息三个领域，揭示现代QA系统在源发现、关系链推理和跨模态合成方面的关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QA系统在干净、模式对齐的语料上表现优异，但现实中的企业数据湖是异构、弱结构化的，包含表格、文本和元数据混杂。当前基准测试忽略了这种噪声发现过程，无法评估端到端性能。

Method: 构建包含9,846个QA对的人工验证基准，每个问题配有多模态精确证据指针。通过隔离源发现与跨模态合成两个阶段，系统评估标准RAG和智能工具使用方法在三个领域的表现。

Result: 高质量检索不保证正确推理。系统在元数据图的关系链推理、银行账本的政策依据推理以及生物医学表格联合问答上持续失败。

Conclusion: 未来智能QA系统需要鲁棒的发现机制和忠实的跨文件合成能力，当前系统在这两方面存在明显不足。

Abstract: While modern question answering (QA) systems excel on clean, schema-aligned corpora, real-world knowledge is rarely so neatly packaged. Answering questions over enterprise and scientific data lakes requires systems to navigate heterogeneous, weakly structured collections of tables, passages, and linked metadata. Current benchmarks abstract away this noisy discovery process, failing to evaluate end-to-end performance. To bridge this gap, we introduce LakeQuest, a human-validated benchmark of 9,846 QA pairs designed to evaluate the end-to-end retrieve-and-synthesize pipeline over realistic data lakes. LakeQuest spans three diverse domains (AI/ML metadata, retail banking, and multimodal biomedical drug information) and pairs every question with exact, modality-aware evidence pointers. By isolating source discovery from cross-modal synthesis, LakeQuest exposes critical failure modes in modern QA systems. Our baseline evaluations, including standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic tool-use methods, reveal that high-quality retrieval does not guarantee correct reasoning. Systems consistently struggle with relation chaining in metadata graphs, policy grounding in bank ledgers, and joint tabular QA in biomedical contexts, highlighting the need for robust discovery and faithful cross-file composition mechanisms in future agentic QA systems.

</details>


### [122] [QUBO-Optimized Evidence Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering with Unconventional Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12334)
*Rahul Singh,Madhav Vadlamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将多跳RAG中的证据选择问题形式化为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，构建平衡相关性、需求覆盖、支持强度、冗余、互补性和紧凑性的能量函数，通过求解器选择最优证据子集，在HotpotQA上达到与LLM选择器相当的性能，同时将LLM解放用于语义处理与答案生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG流水线依赖top-k排序，仅根据单个相关性分数选择段落，无法处理需要互补证据的多跳问题；基于LLM的选择器虽将检索视为集合选择，但成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: 将证据选择建模为QUBO问题，构建能量函数综合评估相关性、需求覆盖、支持强度、冗余惩罚、互补性和紧凑性，通过求解低能量解获得最优证据子集，再交由下游语言模型生成答案。

Result: 在HotpotQA上，QUBO选择器在精确匹配和token-F1指标上与LLM选择器性能相当，优于BM25、top-k、MMR等非LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 多跳证据选择可被形式化为离散优化问题，为将LLM从上下文选择中解放、专注于语义处理与答案生成的RAG流水线提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented question answering depends on selecting evidence passages that jointly support answer generation. However, many RAG pipelines rely on top-\(k\) ranking, where passages are selected mainly by individual relevance scores, even though multi-hop questions often require complementary evidence satisfying multiple information requirements. Recent LLM-based selectors address this by treating retrieval as set selection, but using an LLM for this intermediate stage can be costly and difficult to scale. In this work, we formulate evidence selection as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem. Given a question, candidate passages, and decomposed information requirements, our method constructs an energy function that balances relevance, requirement coverage, support strength, redundancy, complementarity, and compactness. Low-energy solutions correspond to compact evidence subsets that cover the needed requirements while avoiding unnecessary or repetitive context. The selected passages are then passed to a downstream language model for answer generation, separating combinatorial evidence selection from semantic answer generation. We evaluate the proposed QUBO selector on HotpotQA and compare it with LLM-based set selectors and non-LLM baselines including BM25, relevance top-\(k\), maximal marginal relevance, hybrid lexical--semantic ranking, greedy coverage, and random selection. The QUBO selector achieves competitive exact-match and token-F1 performance relative to LLM-based selectors while providing a solver-compatible formulation for structured evidence selection. These results suggest that multi-hop evidence selection can be cast as discrete optimization, opening a path toward RAG pipelines where LLMs are reserved for semantic processing and answer generation, while context selection is handled by Ising/QUBO-compatible solvers.

</details>


### [123] [Evaluating Health Misinformation in Low-Resource Languages: Integrating Small Language Models with a Culturally-Sensitive Responsible NLP Framework (Bangla as a Case Study)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12336)
*Farnaz Farid,Raihan Alam,Al Al-Areqi,Farhad Ahamed,Muhammad Hassan Khan,Sadia Hossain,Irena Veljanova,Anika Tabassum Binte Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向CALD（文化语言多样性）社区的健康 misinformation 检测框架，使用 Bangla 翻译数据集比较多种小语言模型（SLM），发现 Phi-4 在声明提取中表现最优，并设计了融入文化敏感性、潜在危害和沟通质量的责任 NLP 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 工具在非英语语境和低社会经济阶层中因训练数据匮乏而性能不足，无法处理语言细微差别和传统习俗，导致 CALD 社区难以通过 AI 获取可信健康信息。

Method: 使用 Bangla 翻译的健康 misinformation 数据集，对比评估多种 SLM 的性能；针对 SLM 局限，设计基于责任 NLP 的健康 misinformation 检测框架，融入文化敏感性、潜在危害和沟通质量三个维度。

Result: Phi-4 在声明提取任务中实现精确率与召回率的最佳平衡，成为最优模型。

Conclusion: 所提框架为低资源语言环境下的 misinformation 检测提供了更全面的评估视角，是缓解 CALD 群体 misinformation 问题的关键步骤。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) technologies, while serving as a foundational enabler for modern social media and digital health services, exert a bivalent effect by simultaneously acting as a combatant against and a spread vector for misinformation. A prevalent challenge in mitigating this issue arises in non-English contexts and low socioeconomic classes, where limited data hinders the training of AI models for effective detection. Consequently, culturally and linguistically diverse (CALD) communities struggle to access trustworthy health information through AI-driven tools. Current AI tools underperform due to a lack of training data and are largely unable to consider language nuances and traditions in non-English contexts. This research addresses these gaps by proposing a CALD-friendly AI-based health misinformation detector and providing a dashboard for medical professionals to analyse this misinformation, a critical step toward mitigating a growing concern among CALD populations. To this end, we conduct a series of experiments using a Bangla-translated health misinformation dataset to evaluate the performance of various Small Language Models (SLMs). SLMs are particularly relevant in this context given the frequent underperformance of Large Language Models (LLMs), which often stems from insufficient domain-specific knowledge and the prohibitive costs of resource-intensive fine-tuning. The results demonstrate that Phi-4 is the superior model, achieving an ideal balance between precision and recall in claim extraction. Then, to mitigate the limitations of SLMs, we design and test a novel health misinformation detection framework grounded in Responsible Natural Language Processing (NLP), which incorporates cultural sensitivity, potential for harm, and communication quality, thereby providing a holistic lens for evaluating misinformation in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [124] [Policy-Conditioned Constrained Decoding for Column-Level Access Control in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12341)
*Ryoto Miyamoto,Xin Fan,Hayato Yamana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PCC-SQL通过在解码过程中对每个token的logits进行掩码，实现了在单次解码中确定性消除列使用违规，在三个基准测试中达到0%泄露率和最高88.7%的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统通常基于查询提及的列表随机性地执行策略合规性检查，但查询是否合规不仅取决于出现哪些列，还取决于这些列的使用方式（输出、过滤条件、聚合参数），且随机性方法无法确定性排除违规。

Method: 将策略合规性形式化为列使用策略，覆盖语义用法（输出、过滤条件、聚合参数）；通过将每个角色与解码器追踪的语法产生式对齐来集成策略；在单次解码中对每个token的logits进行掩码，确定性排除违反列使用策略的查询。

Result: 在三个基准测试和三个开源模型上，PCC-SQL在Spider-CU上达到0%泄露率和最高88.7%覆盖率，token开销控制在直接提示的+10%以内。

Conclusion: PCC-SQL在保证策略合规性的同时实现了高覆盖率和低开销，为跨信任边界的Text-to-SQL部署提供了一种确定性且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL is increasingly deployed across trust boundaries between data providers and users. Such deployment must balance three competing requirements: policy compliance, answer coverage, and bounded cost. Existing approaches typically decide refusal based on which columns a query mentions and enforce it stochastically. Whether a query is compliant, however, depends not only on which columns appear but on how they are used, and stochastic enforcement cannot deterministically rule out violations.
